Here\'s a summary of the fix.\n\n## Root cause\n\nIn `ManualEntryDialog.show()`, the `TextWatcher` that gated the "Save" button only checked the *Label* and *Secret* fields — the advanced *Period* and *Digits* fields were never validated. The save handler then did an unguarded conversion:\n\n```java\nint period = Integer.parseInt(periodInput.getText().toString());  // "" -> NumberFormatException\nint digits = Integer.parseInt(digitsInput.getText().toString());\n```\n\n- empty field → `NumberFormatException: For input string: ""` (ManualEntryDialog:172)\n- `"0"` → passed through to `Entry.updateOTP()`, where `time / getPeriod()` threw `ArithmeticException: divide by zero` (Entry:300)\n\n## Changes\n\n**`View/ManualEntryDialog.java`** (primary fix — matches the expected behavior in the report)\n- Added a `validateInputs(...)` helper that checks *all four* fields and enables the Save button only when every value is valid. Invalid fields get a short inline `setError(...)` description, as suggested in the report.\n- `isValidNumber()`/`parseInput()` treat empty, non-numeric, `0`, negative, and out-of-range values (incl. overflow like `99999999999`, which also threw `NumberFormatException`) as invalid. Bounds: period `1..300`, digits `1..10`.\n- The watcher is now attached to `periodInput` and `digitsInput` too, plus an initial validation pass so the button state is always consistent (errors are suppressed on that first pass so the dialog doesn\'t open with error popups on untouched fields).\n- The save handler uses the safe `parseInput(...)` with sensible defaults instead of raw `Integer.parseInt`, so it can no longer throw (STEAM entries fall back to `STEAM_DEFAULT_DIGITS`).\n\n**`Database/Entry.java`** (defense in depth — invalid values can also arrive from QR codes or corrupt backup files)\n- `getPeriod()` returns `TOTP_DEFAULT_PERIOD` when `period < 1`, eliminating the divide-by-zero at its source.\n- `getDigits()` returns the type-appropriate default when `digits < 1`.\n- `updateOTP()` now uses `getPeriod()`/`getDigits()` instead of the raw fields, so token calculation uses the sanitized values.\n\n**`res/values/strings_main.xml`** — added three translatable error strings (`error_empty_input`, `error_invalid_period`, `error_invalid_digits`).\n\nBehavior for valid input is unchanged; `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` completes with `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 2415, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 36540, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 2415, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfHhxTmhQA2DX1htnKBhp', stop_reason=None, session_id='a0e8fe7a-5ffb-4291-95c1-a7a33c3b5257', uuid='9c1221cc-ece5-4fc1-b00c-93daaac988a5')
